#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include <mpi.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__) || defined(_MSC_VER)
#define STENCIL_RESTRICT __restrict
#else
#define STENCIL_RESTRICT
#endif

// Initialize only owned planes.  The two extra planes in each local grid are
// receive halos and are populated before every iteration.
void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t planeCells,
                    const size_t localNz, const size_t globalZStart) {
    for (size_t localZ = 0; localZ < localNz; ++localZ) {
        Real* const plane = grid.data() + (localZ + 1) * planeCells;
        const size_t globalOffset = (globalZStart + localZ) * planeCells;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < planeCells; ++i) {
            plane[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalOffset + i) % 19);
        }
    }
}

// Copy the global boundary cells, which retain their values at every step.
// Interior z planes only need their four x/y faces copied.
void copyBoundaryValues(const Real* const input, Real* const output,
                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const size_t localNz, const size_t globalZStart) {
    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
    const size_t rowBytes = nx * sizeof(Real);

    for (size_t localZ = 1; localZ <= localNz; ++localZ) {
        const size_t globalZ = globalZStart + localZ - 1;
        const Real* const inPlane = input + localZ * planeCells;
        Real* const outPlane = output + localZ * planeCells;

        if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == nz) {
            std::memcpy(outPlane, inPlane, planeCells * sizeof(Real));
            continue;
        }

        std::memcpy(outPlane, inPlane, rowBytes);
        std::memcpy(outPlane + (ny - 1) * nx,
                    inPlane + (ny - 1) * nx, rowBytes);
        for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
            const size_t row = y * nx;
            outPlane[row] = inPlane[row];
            outPlane[row + nx - 1] = inPlane[row + nx - 1];
        }
    }
}

// Compute a contiguous set of local z planes.  firstLocalZ and lastLocalZ
// include the local-grid halo offset, so owned planes are [1, localNz].
void stencilInteriorPlanes(const Real* STENCIL_RESTRICT input,
                           Real* STENCIL_RESTRICT output,
                           const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
                           const size_t firstLocalZ,
                           const size_t lastLocalZ) {
    if (firstLocalZ > lastLocalZ) {
        return;
    }

    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
    for (size_t localZ = firstLocalZ; localZ <= lastLocalZ; ++localZ) {
        const Real* const bottom = input + (localZ - 1) * planeCells;
        const Real* const center = input + localZ * planeCells;
        const Real* const top = input + (localZ + 1) * planeCells;
        Real* const out = output + localZ * planeCells;

        for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
            const size_t row = y * nx;
            const Real* const bottomRow = bottom + row;
            const Real* const centerRow = center + row;
            const Real* const topRow = top + row;
            Real* const outRow = out + row;

            for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {
                outRow[x] = (centerRow[x] + centerRow[x - 1] + centerRow[x + 1]
                             + centerRow[x - nx] + centerRow[x + nx]
                             + bottomRow[x] + topRow[x]) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

int beginHaloExchange(std::vector<Real>& input, const size_t localNz,
                      const int planeCount, const int activeRank,
                      const int activeSize, MPI_Comm communicator,
                      MPI_Request requests[4]) {
    const int lowerRank = activeRank > 0 ? activeRank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;
    const int upperRank = activeRank + 1 < activeSize ? activeRank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;
    Real* const firstOwnedPlane = input.data() + planeCount;
    Real* const lastOwnedPlane = input.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localNz) * planeCount;
    int requestCount = 0;

    // A lower neighbour sends its last plane upward with tag 1, while an
    // upper neighbour sends its first plane downward with tag 0.
    if (lowerRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
        MPI_Irecv(input.data(), planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, lowerRank, 1,
                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
        MPI_Isend(firstOwnedPlane, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, lowerRank, 0,
                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
    }
    if (upperRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
        MPI_Irecv(input.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localNz + 1) * planeCount,
                  planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, upperRank, 0, communicator,
                  &requests[requestCount++]);
        MPI_Isend(lastOwnedPlane, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, upperRank, 1,
                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
    }
    return requestCount;
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t localNz,
                    const size_t planeCells, MPI_Comm communicator,
                    const int activeRank) {
    const Real* const owned = grid.data() + planeCells;
    const size_t localCells = localNz * planeCells;
    int localInvalid = 0;
    Real localMin = std::numeric_limits<Real>::max();
    Real localMax = std::numeric_limits<Real>::lowest();

    for (size_t i = 0; i < localCells; ++i) {
        const Real value = owned[i];
        if (!std::isfinite(value)) {
            localInvalid = 1;
        } else {
            localMin = std::min(localMin, value);
            localMax = std::max(localMax, value);
        }
    }

    int invalid = 0;
    Real minValue = 0.0;
    Real maxValue = 0.0;
    MPI_Allreduce(&localInvalid, &invalid, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, communicator);
    MPI_Allreduce(&localMin, &minValue, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, communicator);
    MPI_Allreduce(&localMax, &maxValue, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, communicator);

    if (activeRank == 0 && invalid != 0) {
        std::printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
    }
    if (activeRank == 0) {
        std::printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minValue, maxValue);
    }
    if (minValue < -1e6 || maxValue > 1e6) {
        if (activeRank == 0) {
            std::printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        }
        return false;
    }
    return invalid == 0;
}

void gatherFinalGrid(const std::vector<Real>& localGrid,
                     std::vector<Real>& globalGrid, const size_t nx,
                     const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const size_t localNz,
                     const int activeRank, const int activeSize,
                     MPI_Comm communicator) {
    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
    const int sendCount = static_cast<int>(localNz * planeCells);
    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
    std::vector<int> displacements;

    if (activeRank == 0) {
        globalGrid.resize(nz * planeCells);
        receiveCounts.resize(activeSize);
        displacements.resize(activeSize);

        const size_t basePlanes = nz / static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
        const size_t remainder = nz % static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
        size_t displacement = 0;
        for (int rank = 0; rank < activeSize; ++rank) {
            const size_t rankPlanes = basePlanes
                + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
            receiveCounts[rank] = static_cast<int>(rankPlanes * planeCells);
            displacements[rank] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
            displacement += rankPlanes * planeCells;
        }
    }

    MPI_Gatherv(localGrid.data() + planeCells, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
                activeRank == 0 ? globalGrid.data() : nullptr,
                activeRank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
                activeRank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
                MPI_DOUBLE, 0, communicator);
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    std::printf("Options:\n");
    std::printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    std::printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    std::printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    std::printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);

    int worldRank = 0;
    int worldSize = 0;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);

    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    bool showHelp = false;
    bool parseError = false;

    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = static_cast<size_t>(std::atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = static_cast<size_t>(std::atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = static_cast<size_t>(std::atoi(argv[++i]));
        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = std::atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            showHelp = true;
        } else {
            if (worldRank == 0) {
                std::printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            }
            parseError = true;
        }
    }

    if (showHelp || parseError) {
        if (worldRank == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
        }
        MPI_Finalize();
        return parseError ? 1 : 0;
    }

    if (ny == 0) {
        ny = nx;
    }
    if (nz == 0) {
        nz = nx;
    }

    const bool invalidDimensions = nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3
        || nx > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / ny
        || nx * ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nz;
    if (invalidDimensions || iterations < 0) {
        if (worldRank == 0) {
            std::printf("Grid dimensions must be at least 3 and iterations must be non-negative\n");
        }
        MPI_Finalize();
        return 1;
    }

    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
    const size_t gridCells = planeCells * nz;
    if (planeCells > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())
        || gridCells > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
        if (worldRank == 0) {
            std::printf("Grid is too large for this MPI implementation\n");
        }
        MPI_Finalize();
        return 1;
    }

    const int activeSize = std::min(worldSize, static_cast<int>(nz));
    MPI_Comm activeCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
    const int color = worldRank < activeSize ? 0 : MPI_UNDEFINED;
    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, color, worldRank, &activeCommunicator);

    int exitCode = 0;
    if (activeCommunicator != MPI_COMM_NULL) {
        int activeRank = 0;
        MPI_Comm_rank(activeCommunicator, &activeRank);

        const size_t basePlanes = nz / static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
        const size_t remainder = nz % static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
        const size_t localNz = basePlanes
            + (static_cast<size_t>(activeRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
        const size_t globalZStart = static_cast<size_t>(activeRank) * basePlanes
            + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(activeRank), remainder);
        const size_t localCellsWithHalos = (localNz + 2) * planeCells;

        std::vector<Real> grid1(localCellsWithHalos);
        std::vector<Real> grid2(localCellsWithHalos);

        if (activeRank == 0) {
            std::printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
            std::printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
            std::printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
            std::printf("Initializing grid...\n");
        }
        initializeGrid(grid1, planeCells, localNz, globalZStart);

        if (activeRank == 0) {
            std::printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
        }
        MPI_Barrier(activeCommunicator);
        const double startTime = MPI_Wtime();

        for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
            std::vector<Real>& input = iteration % 2 == 0 ? grid1 : grid2;
            std::vector<Real>& output = iteration % 2 == 0 ? grid2 : grid1;
            MPI_Request requests[4];
            const int requestCount = beginHaloExchange(
                input, localNz, static_cast<int>(planeCells), activeRank,
                activeSize, activeCommunicator, requests);

            copyBoundaryValues(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny, nz,
                               localNz, globalZStart);

            // These planes use only local data, so their work hides halo
            // communication.  The first and last planes are done after wait.
            if (localNz > 2) {
                stencilInteriorPlanes(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny, 2,
                                      localNz - 1);
            }
            if (requestCount != 0) {
                MPI_Waitall(requestCount, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
            }

            if (globalZStart != 0 && globalZStart + 1 != nz) {
                stencilInteriorPlanes(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny, 1, 1);
            }
            if (localNz > 1 && globalZStart + localNz != nz) {
                stencilInteriorPlanes(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny,
                                      localNz, localNz);
            }
        }

        const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - startTime;
        double elapsed = 0.0;
        MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
                   activeCommunicator);

        if (activeRank == 0) {

+// Initialize only owned planes.  The two extra planes in each local grid are
+// receive halos and are populated before every iteration.
+void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t planeCells,
+                    const size_t localNz, const size_t globalZStart) {
+    for (size_t localZ = 0; localZ < localNz; ++localZ) {
+        Real* const plane = grid.data() + (localZ + 1) * planeCells;
+        const size_t globalOffset = (globalZStart + localZ) * planeCells;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < planeCells; ++i) {
+            plane[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalOffset + i) % 19);
+        }
+    }
 }
 
-void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {

+// Copy the global boundary cells, which retain their values at every step.
+// Interior z planes only need their four x/y faces copied.
+void copyBoundaryValues(const Real* const input, Real* const output,
+                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                        const size_t localNz, const size_t globalZStart) {
+    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
+    const size_t rowBytes = nx * sizeof(Real);
+
+    for (size_t localZ = 1; localZ <= localNz; ++localZ) {
+        const size_t globalZ = globalZStart + localZ - 1;
+        const Real* const inPlane = input + localZ * planeCells;
+        Real* const outPlane = output + localZ * planeCells;
+
+        if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == nz) {
+            std::memcpy(outPlane, inPlane, planeCells * sizeof(Real));
+            continue;
         }
+
+        std::memcpy(outPlane, inPlane, rowBytes);
+        std::memcpy(outPlane + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                    inPlane + (ny - 1) * nx, rowBytes);
+        for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+            const size_t row = y * nx;
+            outPlane[row] = inPlane[row];
+            outPlane[row + nx - 1] = inPlane[row + nx - 1];
+        }
     }
 }

+// Compute a contiguous set of local z planes.  firstLocalZ and lastLocalZ
+// include the local-grid halo offset, so owned planes are [1, localNz].
+void stencilInteriorPlanes(const Real* STENCIL_RESTRICT input,
+                           Real* STENCIL_RESTRICT output,
+                           const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                           const size_t firstLocalZ,
+                           const size_t lastLocalZ) {
+    if (firstLocalZ > lastLocalZ) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t localZ = firstLocalZ; localZ <= lastLocalZ; ++localZ) {
+        const Real* const bottom = input + (localZ - 1) * planeCells;
+        const Real* const center = input + localZ * planeCells;
+        const Real* const top = input + (localZ + 1) * planeCells;
+        Real* const out = output + localZ * planeCells;
+
+        for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+            const size_t row = y * nx;
+            const Real* const bottomRow = bottom + row;
+            const Real* const centerRow = center + row;
+            const Real* const topRow = top + row;
+            Real* const outRow = out + row;
+
+            for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {
+                outRow[x] = (centerRow[x] + centerRow[x - 1] + centerRow[x + 1]
+                             + centerRow[x - nx] + centerRow[x + nx]
+                             + bottomRow[x] + topRow[x]) / 7.0;
             }
         }
     }

+int beginHaloExchange(std::vector<Real>& input, const size_t localNz,
+                      const int planeCount, const int activeRank,
+                      const int activeSize, MPI_Comm communicator,
+                      MPI_Request requests[4]) {
+    const int lowerRank = activeRank > 0 ? activeRank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;
+    const int upperRank = activeRank + 1 < activeSize ? activeRank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;
+    Real* const firstOwnedPlane = input.data() + planeCount;
+    Real* const lastOwnedPlane = input.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localNz) * planeCount;
+    int requestCount = 0;
+
+    // A lower neighbour sends its last plane upward with tag 1, while an
+    // upper neighbour sends its first plane downward with tag 0.
+    if (lowerRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data(), planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, lowerRank, 1,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(firstOwnedPlane, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, lowerRank, 0,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
+    }
+    if (upperRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localNz + 1) * planeCount,
+                  planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, upperRank, 0, communicator,
+                  &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(lastOwnedPlane, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, upperRank, 1,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
     }
+    return requestCount;
 }

+void gatherFinalGrid(const std::vector<Real>& localGrid,
+                     std::vector<Real>& globalGrid, const size_t nx,
+                     const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const size_t localNz,
+                     const int activeRank, const int activeSize,
+                     MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
+    const int sendCount = static_cast<int>(localNz * planeCells);
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    if (activeRank == 0) {
+        globalGrid.resize(nz * planeCells);
+        receiveCounts.resize(activeSize);
+        displacements.resize(activeSize);
+
+        const size_t basePlanes = nz / static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
+        const size_t remainder = nz % static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
+        size_t displacement = 0;
+        for (int rank = 0; rank < activeSize; ++rank) {
+            const size_t rankPlanes = basePlanes
+                + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+            receiveCounts[rank] = static_cast<int>(rankPlanes * planeCells);
+            displacements[rank] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+            displacement += rankPlanes * planeCells;
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localGrid.data() + planeCells, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                activeRank == 0 ? globalGrid.data() : nullptr,
+                activeRank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                activeRank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE, 0, communicator);
+}

+        }
+        initializeGrid(grid1, planeCells, localNz, globalZStart);
+
+        if (activeRank == 0) {
+            std::printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
+        }
+        MPI_Barrier(activeCommunicator);
+        const double startTime = MPI_Wtime();
+
+        for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
+            std::vector<Real>& input = iteration % 2 == 0 ? grid1 : grid2;
+            std::vector<Real>& output = iteration % 2 == 0 ? grid2 : grid1;
+            MPI_Request requests[4];
+            const int requestCount = beginHaloExchange(
+                input, localNz, static_cast<int>(planeCells), activeRank,
+                activeSize, activeCommunicator, requests);
+
+            copyBoundaryValues(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny, nz,
+                               localNz, globalZStart);
+
+            // These planes use only local data, so their work hides halo
+            // communication.  The first and last planes are done after wait.
+            if (localNz > 2) {
+                stencilInteriorPlanes(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny, 2,
+                                      localNz - 1);
+            }
+            if (requestCount != 0) {
+                MPI_Waitall(requestCount, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+            }
+
+            if (globalZStart != 0 && globalZStart + 1 != nz) {
+                stencilInteriorPlanes(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny, 1, 1);
+            }
+            if (localNz > 1 && globalZStart + localNz != nz) {
+                stencilInteriorPlanes(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny,
+                                      localNz, localNz);
+            }
+        }
+
+        const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - startTime;
+        double elapsed = 0.0;
+        MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+                   activeCommunicator);
+
+        if (activeRank == 0) {
+            const long milliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
+            const double cellUpdates = static_cast<double>(nx - 2)
+                * static_cast<double>(ny - 2) * static_cast<double>(nz - 2)
+                * static_cast<double>(iterations);
+            const double mcups = elapsed > 0.0 ? cellUpdates / elapsed / 1e6 : 0.0;
+            std::printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", milliseconds);
+            std::printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
         }
+
+        const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = iterations % 2 == 0 ? grid1 : grid2;
+        if (printResults) {
+            std::vector<Real> gatheredGrid;
+            gatherFinalGrid(finalGrid, gatheredGrid, nx, ny, nz, localNz,
+                            activeRank, activeSize, activeCommunicator);
+            if (activeRank == 0) {
+                print_results(gatheredGrid, "Grid");
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (validate) {
+            if (activeRank == 0) {
+                std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            }
+            if (validateResult(finalGrid, localNz, planeCells,
+                               activeCommunicator, activeRank)) {
+                if (activeRank == 0) {
+                    std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+                }
+            } else {
+                if (activeRank == 0) {
+                    std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                }
+                exitCode = 1;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Comm_free(&activeCommunicator);
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return exitCode;
 }

+// Initialize only owned planes.  The two extra planes in each local grid are
+// receive halos and are populated before every iteration.
+void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t planeCells,
+                    const size_t localNz, const size_t globalZStart) {
+    for (size_t localZ = 0; localZ < localNz; ++localZ) {
+        Real* const plane = grid.data() + (localZ + 1) * planeCells;
+        const size_t globalOffset = (globalZStart + localZ) * planeCells;

+            plane[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalOffset + i) % 19);
+        }
+    }

+// Copy the global boundary cells, which retain their values at every step.
+// Interior z planes only need their four x/y faces copied.
+void copyBoundaryValues(const Real* const input, Real* const output,
+                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                        const size_t localNz, const size_t globalZStart) {
+    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
+    const size_t rowBytes = nx * sizeof(Real);
+
+    for (size_t localZ = 1; localZ <= localNz; ++localZ) {
+        const size_t globalZ = globalZStart + localZ - 1;
+        const Real* const inPlane = input + localZ * planeCells;
+        Real* const outPlane = output + localZ * planeCells;
+
+        if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == nz) {
+            std::memcpy(outPlane, inPlane, planeCells * sizeof(Real));
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        std::memcpy(outPlane, inPlane, rowBytes);
+        std::memcpy(outPlane + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                    inPlane + (ny - 1) * nx, rowBytes);
+        for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+            const size_t row = y * nx;
+            outPlane[row] = inPlane[row];
+            outPlane[row + nx - 1] = inPlane[row + nx - 1];
         }
     }
 }

+void stencilInteriorPlanes(const Real* STENCIL_RESTRICT input,

+                           const size_t nx, const size_t ny,

+        const Real* const top = input + (localZ + 1) * planeCells;
+        Real* const out = output + localZ * planeCells;

+int beginHaloExchange(std::vector<Real>& input, const size_t localNz,
+                      const int planeCount, const int activeRank,
+                      const int activeSize, MPI_Comm communicator,
+                      MPI_Request requests[4]) {
+    const int lowerRank = activeRank > 0 ? activeRank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;
+    const int upperRank = activeRank + 1 < activeSize ? activeRank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;
+    Real* const firstOwnedPlane = input.data() + planeCount;
+    Real* const lastOwnedPlane = input.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localNz) * planeCount;
+    int requestCount = 0;
+
+    // A lower neighbour sends its last plane upward with tag 1, while an
+    // upper neighbour sends its first plane downward with tag 0.
+    if (lowerRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data(), planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, lowerRank, 1,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(firstOwnedPlane, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, lowerRank, 0,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
+    }
+    if (upperRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localNz + 1) * planeCount,
+                  planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, upperRank, 0, communicator,
+                  &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(lastOwnedPlane, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, upperRank, 1,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
     }
+    return requestCount;
 }

+void gatherFinalGrid(const std::vector<Real>& localGrid,
+                     std::vector<Real>& globalGrid, const size_t nx,
+                     const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const size_t localNz,
+                     const int activeRank, const int activeSize,
+                     MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
+    const int sendCount = static_cast<int>(localNz * planeCells);
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    if (activeRank == 0) {
+        globalGrid.resize(nz * planeCells);
+        receiveCounts.resize(activeSize);
+        displacements.resize(activeSize);
+
+        const size_t basePlanes = nz / static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
+        const size_t remainder = nz % static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
+        size_t displacement = 0;
+        for (int rank = 0; rank < activeSize; ++rank) {
+            const size_t rankPlanes = basePlanes
+                + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+            receiveCounts[rank] = static_cast<int>(rankPlanes * planeCells);
+            displacements[rank] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+            displacement += rankPlanes * planeCells;
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localGrid.data() + planeCells, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                activeRank == 0 ? globalGrid.data() : nullptr,
+                activeRank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                activeRank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE, 0, communicator);
 }

+    MPI_Comm activeCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    const int color = worldRank < activeSize ? 0 : MPI_UNDEFINED;
+    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, color, worldRank, &activeCommunicator);
+
+    int exitCode = 0;
+    if (activeCommunicator != MPI_COMM_NULL) {
+        int activeRank = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(activeCommunicator, &activeRank);
+
+        const size_t basePlanes = nz / static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
+        const size_t remainder = nz % static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
+        const size_t localNz = basePlanes
+            + (static_cast<size_t>(activeRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        const size_t globalZStart = static_cast<size_t>(activeRank) * basePlanes
+            + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(activeRank), remainder);
+        const size_t localCellsWithHalos = (localNz + 2) * planeCells;
+
+        std::vector<Real> grid1(localCellsWithHalos);
+        std::vector<Real> grid2(localCellsWithHalos);
+
+        if (activeRank == 0) {
+            std::printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
+            std::printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
+            std::printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
+            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+            std::printf("Initializing grid...\n");
+        }
+        initializeGrid(grid1, planeCells, localNz, globalZStart);
+
+        if (activeRank == 0) {
+            std::printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
+        }
+        MPI_Barrier(activeCommunicator);
+        const double startTime = MPI_Wtime();
+
+        for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
+            std::vector<Real>& input = iteration % 2 == 0 ? grid1 : grid2;
+            std::vector<Real>& output = iteration % 2 == 0 ? grid2 : grid1;
+            MPI_Request requests[4];
+            const int requestCount = beginHaloExchange(
+                input, localNz, static_cast<int>(planeCells), activeRank,
+                activeSize, activeCommunicator, requests);
+
+            copyBoundaryValues(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny, nz,
+                               localNz, globalZStart);
+
+            // These planes use only local data, so their work hides halo
+            // communication.  The first and last planes are done after wait.
+            if (localNz > 2) {
+                stencilInteriorPlanes(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny, 2,
+                                      localNz - 1);
+            }
+            if (requestCount != 0) {
+                MPI_Waitall(requestCount, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+            }
+
+            if (globalZStart != 0 && globalZStart + 1 != nz) {
+                stencilInteriorPlanes(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny, 1, 1);
+            }
+            if (localNz > 1 && globalZStart + localNz != nz) {
+                stencilInteriorPlanes(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny,
+                                      localNz, localNz);
+            }
+        }
+
+        const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - startTime;
+        double elapsed = 0.0;
+        MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+                   activeCommunicator);
+
+        if (activeRank == 0) {
+            const long milliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
+            const double cellUpdates = static_cast<double>(nx - 2)
+                * static_cast<double>(ny - 2) * static_cast<double>(nz - 2)
+                * static_cast<double>(iterations);
+            const double mcups = elapsed > 0.0 ? cellUpdates / elapsed / 1e6 : 0.0;
+            std::printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", milliseconds);
+            std::printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
+        }
+
+        const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = iterations % 2 == 0 ? grid1 : grid2;
+        if (printResults) {
+            std::vector<Real> gatheredGrid;
+            gatherFinalGrid(finalGrid, gatheredGrid, nx, ny, nz, localNz,
+                            activeRank, activeSize, activeCommunicator);
+            if (activeRank == 0) {
+                print_results(gatheredGrid, "Grid");
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (validate) {
+            if (activeRank == 0) {
+                std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            }
+            if (validateResult(finalGrid, localNz, planeCells,
+                               activeCommunicator, activeRank)) {
+                if (activeRank == 0) {
+                    std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+                }
+            } else {
+                if (activeRank == 0) {
+                    std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                }
+                exitCode = 1;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Comm_free(&activeCommunicator);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return exitCode;
 }

+#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__) || defined(_MSC_VER)
+#define STENCIL_RESTRICT __restrict

+// Initialize only owned planes.  The two extra planes in each local grid are
+// receive halos and are populated before every iteration.
+void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t planeCells,
+                    const size_t localNz, const size_t globalZStart) {
+    for (size_t localZ = 0; localZ < localNz; ++localZ) {
+        Real* const plane = grid.data() + (localZ + 1) * planeCells;
+        const size_t globalOffset = (globalZStart + localZ) * planeCells;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < planeCells; ++i) {
+            plane[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalOffset + i) % 19);
+        }
+    }
 }
 
-void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {

+// Copy the global boundary cells, which retain their values at every step.
+// Interior z planes only need their four x/y faces copied.
+void copyBoundaryValues(const Real* const input, Real* const output,
+                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                        const size_t localNz, const size_t globalZStart) {
+    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
+    const size_t rowBytes = nx * sizeof(Real);
+
+    for (size_t localZ = 1; localZ <= localNz; ++localZ) {
+        const size_t globalZ = globalZStart + localZ - 1;
+        const Real* const inPlane = input + localZ * planeCells;
+        Real* const outPlane = output + localZ * planeCells;
+
+        if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == nz) {
+            std::memcpy(outPlane, inPlane, planeCells * sizeof(Real));
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        std::memcpy(outPlane, inPlane, rowBytes);
+        std::memcpy(outPlane + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                    inPlane + (ny - 1) * nx, rowBytes);
+        for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+            const size_t row = y * nx;
+            outPlane[row] = inPlane[row];
+            outPlane[row + nx - 1] = inPlane[row + nx - 1];
         }
     }
 }

+// Compute a contiguous set of local z planes.  firstLocalZ and lastLocalZ

+void stencilInteriorPlanes(const Real* STENCIL_RESTRICT input,
+                           Real* STENCIL_RESTRICT output,
+                           const size_t nx, const size_t ny,

+        const Real* const center = input + localZ * planeCells;
+        const Real* const top = input + (localZ + 1) * planeCells;
+        Real* const out = output + localZ * planeCells;
+

+            const Real* const centerRow = center + row;
+            const Real* const topRow = top + row;
+            Real* const outRow = out + row;

+                             + centerRow[x - nx] + centerRow[x + nx]
+                             + bottomRow[x] + topRow[x]) / 7.0;

         }
     }

+int beginHaloExchange(std::vector<Real>& input, const size_t localNz,
+                      const int planeCount, const int activeRank,
+                      const int activeSize, MPI_Comm communicator,
+                      MPI_Request requests[4]) {
+    const int lowerRank = activeRank > 0 ? activeRank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;
+    const int upperRank = activeRank + 1 < activeSize ? activeRank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;
+    Real* const firstOwnedPlane = input.data() + planeCount;
+    Real* const lastOwnedPlane = input.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localNz) * planeCount;
+    int requestCount = 0;
+
+    // A lower neighbour sends its last plane upward with tag 1, while an
+    // upper neighbour sends its first plane downward with tag 0.
+    if (lowerRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data(), planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, lowerRank, 1,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(firstOwnedPlane, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, lowerRank, 0,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
+    }
+    if (upperRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localNz + 1) * planeCount,
+                  planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, upperRank, 0, communicator,
+                  &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(lastOwnedPlane, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, upperRank, 1,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
     }
+    return requestCount;
 }

+
+void gatherFinalGrid(const std::vector<Real>& localGrid,
+                     std::vector<Real>& globalGrid, const size_t nx,
+                     const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const size_t localNz,
+                     const int activeRank, const int activeSize,
+                     MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
+    const int sendCount = static_cast<int>(localNz * planeCells);
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    if (activeRank == 0) {
+        globalGrid.resize(nz * planeCells);
+        receiveCounts.resize(activeSize);
+        displacements.resize(activeSize);
+
+        const size_t basePlanes = nz / static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
+        const size_t remainder = nz % static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
+        size_t displacement = 0;
+        for (int rank = 0; rank < activeSize; ++rank) {
+            const size_t rankPlanes = basePlanes
+                + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+            receiveCounts[rank] = static_cast<int>(rankPlanes * planeCells);
+            displacements[rank] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+            displacement += rankPlanes * planeCells;
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localGrid.data() + planeCells, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                activeRank == 0 ? globalGrid.data() : nullptr,
+                activeRank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                activeRank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE, 0, communicator);
 }

-    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
-    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
-    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
+    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
+    std::printf("Options:\n");
+    std::printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
+    std::printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
+    std::printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");

+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
 }

+            std::printf("A grid plane is too large for this MPI implementation\n");
+        }
+        MPI_Finalize();
+        return 1;
     }
-    
-    // Validation
-    if (validate) {
-        printf("Validating result...\n");
-        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
-        
-        if (valid) {
-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;
+    if (printResults && gridCells > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        if (worldRank == 0) {
+            std::printf("Grid is too large to gather for result printing\n");
         }
+        MPI_Finalize();
+        return 1;
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    const int activeSize = nz < static_cast<size_t>(worldSize)
+        ? static_cast<int>(nz) : worldSize;
+    MPI_Comm activeCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    const int color = worldRank < activeSize ? 0 : MPI_UNDEFINED;
+    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, color, worldRank, &activeCommunicator);
+
+    int exitCode = 0;
+    if (activeCommunicator != MPI_COMM_NULL) {
+        int activeRank = 0;
+        MPI_Comm_rank(activeCommunicator, &activeRank);
+
+        const size_t basePlanes = nz / static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
+        const size_t remainder = nz % static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
+        const size_t localNz = basePlanes
+            + (static_cast<size_t>(activeRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        const size_t globalZStart = static_cast<size_t>(activeRank) * basePlanes
+            + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(activeRank), remainder);
+        const size_t localCellsWithHalos = (localNz + 2) * planeCells;
+
+        std::vector<Real> grid1(localCellsWithHalos);
+        std::vector<Real> grid2(localCellsWithHalos);
+
+        if (activeRank == 0) {
+            std::printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
+            std::printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
+            std::printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
+            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+            std::printf("Initializing grid...\n");
+        }
+        initializeGrid(grid1, planeCells, localNz, globalZStart);
+
+        if (activeRank == 0) {
+            std::printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
+        }
+        MPI_Barrier(activeCommunicator);
+        const double startTime = MPI_Wtime();
+
+        for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
+            std::vector<Real>& input = iteration % 2 == 0 ? grid1 : grid2;
+            std::vector<Real>& output = iteration % 2 == 0 ? grid2 : grid1;
+            MPI_Request requests[4];
+            const int requestCount = beginHaloExchange(
+                input, localNz, static_cast<int>(planeCells), activeRank,
+                activeSize, activeCommunicator, requests);
+
+            copyBoundaryValues(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny, nz,
+                               localNz, globalZStart);
+
+            // These planes use only local data, so their work hides halo
+            // communication.  The first and last planes are done after wait.
+            if (localNz > 2) {
+                stencilInteriorPlanes(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny, 2,
+                                      localNz - 1);
+            }
+            if (requestCount != 0) {
+                MPI_Waitall(requestCount, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+            }
+
+            if (globalZStart != 0 && globalZStart + 1 != nz) {
+                stencilInteriorPlanes(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny, 1, 1);
+            }
+            if (localNz > 1 && globalZStart + localNz != nz) {
+                stencilInteriorPlanes(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny,
+                                      localNz, localNz);
+            }
+        }
+
+        const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - startTime;
+        double elapsed = 0.0;
+        MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+                   activeCommunicator);
+
+        if (activeRank == 0) {
+            const long milliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
+            const double cellUpdates = static_cast<double>(nx - 2)
+                * static_cast<double>(ny - 2) * static_cast<double>(nz - 2)
+                * static_cast<double>(iterations);
+            const double mcups = elapsed > 0.0 ? cellUpdates / elapsed / 1e6 : 0.0;
+            std::printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", milliseconds);
+            std::printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
+        }
+
+        const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = iterations % 2 == 0 ? grid1 : grid2;
+        if (printResults) {
+            std::vector<Real> gatheredGrid;
+            gatherFinalGrid(finalGrid, gatheredGrid, nx, ny, nz, localNz,
+                            activeRank, activeSize, activeCommunicator);
+            if (activeRank == 0) {
+                print_results(gatheredGrid, "Grid");
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (validate) {
+            if (activeRank == 0) {
+                std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            }
+            if (validateResult(finalGrid, localNz, planeCells,
+                               activeCommunicator, activeRank)) {
+                if (activeRank == 0) {
+                    std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+                }
+            } else {
+                if (activeRank == 0) {
+                    std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                }
+                exitCode = 1;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Comm_free(&activeCommunicator);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return exitCode;
 }

+// Initialize only owned planes.  The two extra planes in each local grid are

+void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t planeCells,
+                    const size_t localNz, const size_t globalZStart) {
+    for (size_t localZ = 0; localZ < localNz; ++localZ) {

+        const size_t globalOffset = (globalZStart + localZ) * planeCells;

+            plane[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalOffset + i) % 19);

+// Copy the global boundary cells, which retain their values at every step.
+// Interior z planes only need their four x/y faces copied.
+void copyBoundaryValues(const Real* const input, Real* const output,
+                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                        const size_t localNz, const size_t globalZStart) {
+    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
+    const size_t rowBytes = nx * sizeof(Real);
+
+    for (size_t localZ = 1; localZ <= localNz; ++localZ) {
+        const size_t globalZ = globalZStart + localZ - 1;
+        const Real* const inPlane = input + localZ * planeCells;
+        Real* const outPlane = output + localZ * planeCells;
+
+        if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == nz) {
+            std::memcpy(outPlane, inPlane, planeCells * sizeof(Real));
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        std::memcpy(outPlane, inPlane, rowBytes);
+        std::memcpy(outPlane + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                    inPlane + (ny - 1) * nx, rowBytes);
+        for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+            const size_t row = y * nx;
+            outPlane[row] = inPlane[row];
+            outPlane[row + nx - 1] = inPlane[row + nx - 1];
         }
     }
 }

+void stencilInteriorPlanes(const Real* STENCIL_RESTRICT input,

+        const Real* const top = input + (localZ + 1) * planeCells;
+        Real* const out = output + localZ * planeCells;

+int beginHaloExchange(std::vector<Real>& input, const size_t localNz,
+                      const int planeCount, const int activeRank,
+                      const int activeSize, MPI_Comm communicator,
+                      MPI_Request requests[4]) {
+    const int lowerRank = activeRank > 0 ? activeRank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;
+    const int upperRank = activeRank + 1 < activeSize ? activeRank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;
+    Real* const firstOwnedPlane = input.data() + planeCount;
+    Real* const lastOwnedPlane = input.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localNz) * planeCount;
+    int requestCount = 0;
+
+    // A lower neighbour sends its last plane upward with tag 1, while an
+    // upper neighbour sends its first plane downward with tag 0.
+    if (lowerRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data(), planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, lowerRank, 1,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(firstOwnedPlane, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, lowerRank, 0,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
+    }
+    if (upperRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localNz + 1) * planeCount,
+                  planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, upperRank, 0, communicator,
+                  &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(lastOwnedPlane, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, upperRank, 1,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
     }
+    return requestCount;
 }

+bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t localNz,

+void gatherFinalGrid(const std::vector<Real>& localGrid,
+                     std::vector<Real>& globalGrid, const size_t nx,
+                     const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const size_t localNz,
+                     const int activeRank, const int activeSize,
+                     MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
+    const int sendCount = static_cast<int>(localNz * planeCells);
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    if (activeRank == 0) {
+        globalGrid.resize(nz * planeCells);
+        receiveCounts.resize(activeSize);
+        displacements.resize(activeSize);
+
+        const size_t basePlanes = nz / static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
+        const size_t remainder = nz % static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
+        size_t displacement = 0;
+        for (int rank = 0; rank < activeSize; ++rank) {
+            const size_t rankPlanes = basePlanes
+                + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+            receiveCounts[rank] = static_cast<int>(rankPlanes * planeCells);
+            displacements[rank] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+            displacement += rankPlanes * planeCells;
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localGrid.data() + planeCells, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                activeRank == 0 ? globalGrid.data() : nullptr,
+                activeRank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                activeRank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE, 0, communicator);
 }

codex
I also removed an unnecessary whole-grid MPI-count limit from normal runs: only a single z-plane must fit an MPI count; the full-grid limit now applies solely to `-r`, which intentionally gathers the entire result to rank 0.

    13	using Real = double;
    14	
    15	#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__) || defined(_MSC_VER)
    16	#define STENCIL_RESTRICT __restrict
    17	#else
    18	#define STENCIL_RESTRICT
    19	#endif
    20	
    21	// Initialize only owned planes.  The two extra planes in each local grid are
    22	// receive halos and are populated before every iteration.
    23	void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t planeCells,
    24	                    const size_t localNz, const size_t globalZStart) {
    25	    for (size_t localZ = 0; localZ < localNz; ++localZ) {
    26	        Real* const plane = grid.data() + (localZ + 1) * planeCells;
    27	        const size_t globalOffset = (globalZStart + localZ) * planeCells;
    28	        for (size_t i = 0; i < planeCells; ++i) {
    29	            plane[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalOffset + i) % 19);
    30	        }
    31	    }
    32	}
    33	
    34	// Copy the global boundary cells, which retain their values at every step.
    35	// Interior z planes only need their four x/y faces copied.
    36	void copyBoundaryValues(const Real* const input, Real* const output,
    37	                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
    38	                        const size_t localNz, const size_t globalZStart) {
    39	    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
    40	    const size_t rowBytes = nx * sizeof(Real);
    41	
    42	    for (size_t localZ = 1; localZ <= localNz; ++localZ) {
    43	        const size_t globalZ = globalZStart + localZ - 1;
    44	        const Real* const inPlane = input + localZ * planeCells;
    45	        Real* const outPlane = output + localZ * planeCells;
    46	
    47	        if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == nz) {
    48	            std::memcpy(outPlane, inPlane, planeCells * sizeof(Real));
    49	            continue;
    50	        }
    51	
    52	        std::memcpy(outPlane, inPlane, rowBytes);
    53	        std::memcpy(outPlane + (ny - 1) * nx,
    54	                    inPlane + (ny - 1) * nx, rowBytes);
    55	        for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
    56	            const size_t row = y * nx;
    57	            outPlane[row] = inPlane[row];
    58	            outPlane[row + nx - 1] = inPlane[row + nx - 1];
    59	        }
    60	    }
    61	}
    62	
    63	// Compute a contiguous set of local z planes.  firstLocalZ and lastLocalZ
    64	// include the local-grid halo offset, so owned planes are [1, localNz].
    65	void stencilInteriorPlanes(const Real* STENCIL_RESTRICT input,
    66	                           Real* STENCIL_RESTRICT output,
    67	                           const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
    68	                           const size_t firstLocalZ,
    69	                           const size_t lastLocalZ) {
    70	    if (firstLocalZ > lastLocalZ) {
    71	        return;
    72	    }
    73	
    74	    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
    75	    for (size_t localZ = firstLocalZ; localZ <= lastLocalZ; ++localZ) {
    76	        const Real* const bottom = input + (localZ - 1) * planeCells;
    77	        const Real* const center = input + localZ * planeCells;
    78	        const Real* const top = input + (localZ + 1) * planeCells;
    79	        Real* const out = output + localZ * planeCells;
    80	
    81	        for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
    82	            const size_t row = y * nx;
    83	            const Real* const bottomRow = bottom + row;
    84	            const Real* const centerRow = center + row;
    85	            const Real* const topRow = top + row;
    86	            Real* const outRow = out + row;
    87	
    88	            for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {
    89	                outRow[x] = (centerRow[x] + centerRow[x - 1] + centerRow[x + 1]
    90	                             + centerRow[x - nx] + centerRow[x + nx]
    91	                             + bottomRow[x] + topRow[x]) / 7.0;
    92	            }
    93	        }
    94	    }
    95	}
    96	
    97	int beginHaloExchange(std::vector<Real>& input, const size_t localNz,
    98	                      const int planeCount, const int activeRank,
    99	                      const int activeSize, MPI_Comm communicator,
   100	                      MPI_Request requests[4]) {
   101	    const int lowerRank = activeRank > 0 ? activeRank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;
   102	    const int upperRank = activeRank + 1 < activeSize ? activeRank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;
   103	    Real* const firstOwnedPlane = input.data() + planeCount;
   104	    Real* const lastOwnedPlane = input.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localNz) * planeCount;
   105	    int requestCount = 0;
   106	
   107	    // A lower neighbour sends its last plane upward with tag 1, while an
   108	    // upper neighbour sends its first plane downward with tag 0.
   109	    if (lowerRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   110	        MPI_Irecv(input.data(), planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, lowerRank, 1,
   111	                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
   112	        MPI_Isend(firstOwnedPlane, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, lowerRank, 0,
   113	                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
   114	    }
   115	    if (upperRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   116	        MPI_Irecv(input.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localNz + 1) * planeCount,
   117	                  planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, upperRank, 0, communicator,
   118	                  &requests[requestCount++]);
   119	        MPI_Isend(lastOwnedPlane, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, upperRank, 1,
   120	                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
   121	    }
   122	    return requestCount;
   123	}
   124	
   125	bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t localNz,
   126	                    const size_t planeCells, MPI_Comm communicator,
   127	                    const int activeRank) {
   128	    const Real* const owned = grid.data() + planeCells;
   129	    const size_t localCells = localNz * planeCells;
   130	    int localInvalid = 0;
   131	    Real localMin = std::numeric_limits<Real>::max();
   132	    Real localMax = std::numeric_limits<Real>::lowest();
   133	
   134	    for (size_t i = 0; i < localCells; ++i) {
   135	        const Real value = owned[i];
   136	        if (!std::isfinite(value)) {
   137	            localInvalid = 1;
   138	        } else {
   139	            localMin = std::min(localMin, value);
   140	            localMax = std::max(localMax, value);
   141	        }
   142	    }
   143	
   144	    int invalid = 0;
   145	    Real minValue = 0.0;
   146	    Real maxValue = 0.0;
   147	    MPI_Allreduce(&localInvalid, &invalid, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, communicator);
   148	    MPI_Allreduce(&localMin, &minValue, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, communicator);
   149	    MPI_Allreduce(&localMax, &maxValue, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, communicator);
   150	
   151	    if (activeRank == 0 && invalid != 0) {
   152	        std::printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
   153	    }
   154	    if (activeRank == 0) {
   155	        std::printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minValue, maxValue);
   156	    }
   157	    if (minValue < -1e6 || maxValue > 1e6) {
   158	        if (activeRank == 0) {
   159	            std::printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
   160	        }
   161	        return false;
   162	    }
   163	    return invalid == 0;
   164	}
   165	
   166	void gatherFinalGrid(const std::vector<Real>& localGrid,
   167	                     std::vector<Real>& globalGrid, const size_t nx,
   168	                     const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const size_t localNz,
   169	                     const int activeRank, const int activeSize,
   170	                     MPI_Comm communicator) {
   171	    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
   172	    const int sendCount = static_cast<int>(localNz * planeCells);
   173	    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
   174	    std::vector<int> displacements;
   175	
   176	    if (activeRank == 0) {
   177	        globalGrid.resize(nz * planeCells);
   178	        receiveCounts.resize(activeSize);
   179	        displacements.resize(activeSize);
   180	
   181	        const size_t basePlanes = nz / static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
   182	        const size_t remainder = nz % static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
   183	        size_t displacement = 0;
   184	        for (int rank = 0; rank < activeSize; ++rank) {
   185	            const size_t rankPlanes = basePlanes
   186	                + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
   187	            receiveCounts[rank] = static_cast<int>(rankPlanes * planeCells);
   188	            displacements[rank] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
   189	            displacement += rankPlanes * planeCells;
   190	        }
   191	    }
   192	
   193	    MPI_Gatherv(localGrid.data() + planeCells, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
   194	                activeRank == 0 ? globalGrid.data() : nullptr,
   195	                activeRank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
   196	                activeRank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
   197	                MPI_DOUBLE, 0, communicator);
   198	}
   199	
   200	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   201	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   202	    std::printf("Options:\n");
   203	    std::printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
   204	    std::printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
   205	    std::printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
   206	    std::printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
   207	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   208	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   209	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   210	}
   211	
   212	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   213	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   214	
   215	    int worldRank = 0;
   216	    int worldSize = 0;
   217	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);
   218	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
   219	
   220	    size_t nx = 128;
   221	    size_t ny = 0;
   222	    size_t nz = 0;
   223	    int iterations = 10;
   224	    bool validate = false;
   225	    bool printResults = false;
   226	    bool showHelp = false;
   227	    bool parseError = false;
   228	
   229	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   230	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   231	            nx = static_cast<size_t>(std::atoi(argv[++i]));
   232	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   233	            ny = static_cast<size_t>(std::atoi(argv[++i]));
   234	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   235	            nz = static_cast<size_t>(std::atoi(argv[++i]));
   236	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   237	            iterations = std::atoi(argv[++i]);
   238	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   239	            validate = true;
   240	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   241	            printResults = true;
   242	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   243	            showHelp = true;
   244	        } else {
   245	            if (worldRank == 0) {
   246	                std::printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   247	            }
   248	            parseError = true;
   249	        }
   250	    }
   251	
   252	    if (showHelp || parseError) {
   253	        if (worldRank == 0) {
   254	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   255	        }
   256	        MPI_Finalize();
   257	        return parseError ? 1 : 0;
   258	    }
   259	
   260	    if (ny == 0) {
   261	        ny = nx;
   262	    }
   263	    if (nz == 0) {
   264	        nz = nx;
   265	    }
   266	
   267	    const bool invalidDimensions = nx < 3 || ny < 3 || nz < 3
   268	        || nx > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / ny
   269	        || nx * ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nz;
   270	    if (invalidDimensions || iterations < 0) {
   271	        if (worldRank == 0) {
   272	            std::printf("Grid dimensions must be at least 3 and iterations must be non-negative\n");
   273	        }
   274	        MPI_Finalize();
   275	        return 1;
   276	    }
   277	
   278	    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
   279	    const size_t gridCells = planeCells * nz;
   280	    if (planeCells > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   281	        if (worldRank == 0) {
   282	            std::printf("A grid plane is too large for this MPI implementation\n");
   283	        }
   284	        MPI_Finalize();
   285	        return 1;
   286	    }
   287	    if (printResults && gridCells > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   288	        if (worldRank == 0) {
   289	            std::printf("Grid is too large to gather for result printing\n");
   290	        }
   291	        MPI_Finalize();
   292	        return 1;
   293	    }
   294	
   295	    const int activeSize = nz < static_cast<size_t>(worldSize)
   296	        ? static_cast<int>(nz) : worldSize;
   297	    MPI_Comm activeCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   298	    const int color = worldRank < activeSize ? 0 : MPI_UNDEFINED;
   299	    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, color, worldRank, &activeCommunicator);
   300	
   301	    int exitCode = 0;
   302	    if (activeCommunicator != MPI_COMM_NULL) {
   303	        int activeRank = 0;
   304	        MPI_Comm_rank(activeCommunicator, &activeRank);
   305	
   306	        const size_t basePlanes = nz / static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
   307	        const size_t remainder = nz % static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
   308	        const size_t localNz = basePlanes
   309	            + (static_cast<size_t>(activeRank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
   310	        const size_t globalZStart = static_cast<size_t>(activeRank) * basePlanes
   311	            + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(activeRank), remainder);
   312	        const size_t localCellsWithHalos = (localNz + 2) * planeCells;
   313	
   314	        std::vector<Real> grid1(localCellsWithHalos);
   315	        std::vector<Real> grid2(localCellsWithHalos);
   316

+// Initialize only owned planes.  The two extra planes in each local grid are
+// receive halos and are populated before every iteration.
+void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t planeCells,
+                    const size_t localNz, const size_t globalZStart) {
+    for (size_t localZ = 0; localZ < localNz; ++localZ) {
+        Real* const plane = grid.data() + (localZ + 1) * planeCells;
+        const size_t globalOffset = (globalZStart + localZ) * planeCells;
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < planeCells; ++i) {
+            plane[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalOffset + i) % 19);
+        }
+    }
 }
 
-void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {

+// Copy the global boundary cells, which retain their values at every step.
+// Interior z planes only need their four x/y faces copied.
+void copyBoundaryValues(const Real* const input, Real* const output,
+                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                        const size_t localNz, const size_t globalZStart) {
+    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
+    const size_t rowBytes = nx * sizeof(Real);
+
+    for (size_t localZ = 1; localZ <= localNz; ++localZ) {
+        const size_t globalZ = globalZStart + localZ - 1;
+        const Real* const inPlane = input + localZ * planeCells;
+        Real* const outPlane = output + localZ * planeCells;
+
+        if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == nz) {
+            std::memcpy(outPlane, inPlane, planeCells * sizeof(Real));
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        std::memcpy(outPlane, inPlane, rowBytes);
+        std::memcpy(outPlane + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                    inPlane + (ny - 1) * nx, rowBytes);
+        for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+            const size_t row = y * nx;
+            outPlane[row] = inPlane[row];
+            outPlane[row + nx - 1] = inPlane[row + nx - 1];
         }
     }
 }

+// Compute a contiguous set of local z planes.  firstLocalZ and lastLocalZ
+// include the local-grid halo offset, so owned planes are [1, localNz].
+void stencilInteriorPlanes(const Real* STENCIL_RESTRICT input,
+                           Real* STENCIL_RESTRICT output,
+                           const size_t nx, const size_t ny,
+                           const size_t firstLocalZ,
+                           const size_t lastLocalZ) {
+    if (firstLocalZ > lastLocalZ) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
+    for (size_t localZ = firstLocalZ; localZ <= lastLocalZ; ++localZ) {
+        const Real* const bottom = input + (localZ - 1) * planeCells;
+        const Real* const center = input + localZ * planeCells;
+        const Real* const top = input + (localZ + 1) * planeCells;
+        Real* const out = output + localZ * planeCells;
+
+        for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+            const size_t row = y * nx;
+            const Real* const bottomRow = bottom + row;
+            const Real* const centerRow = center + row;
+            const Real* const topRow = top + row;
+            Real* const outRow = out + row;

+            for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {

+int beginHaloExchange(std::vector<Real>& input, const size_t localNz,
+                      const int planeCount, const int activeRank,
+                      const int activeSize, MPI_Comm communicator,
+                      MPI_Request requests[4]) {
+    const int lowerRank = activeRank > 0 ? activeRank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;
+    const int upperRank = activeRank + 1 < activeSize ? activeRank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;
+    Real* const firstOwnedPlane = input.data() + planeCount;
+    Real* const lastOwnedPlane = input.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localNz) * planeCount;
+    int requestCount = 0;
+
+    // A lower neighbour sends its last plane upward with tag 1, while an
+    // upper neighbour sends its first plane downward with tag 0.
+    if (lowerRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data(), planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, lowerRank, 1,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(firstOwnedPlane, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, lowerRank, 0,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
+    }
+    if (upperRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localNz + 1) * planeCount,
+                  planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, upperRank, 0, communicator,
+                  &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(lastOwnedPlane, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, upperRank, 1,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
     }
+    return requestCount;
 }

+void gatherFinalGrid(const std::vector<Real>& localGrid,
+                     std::vector<Real>& globalGrid, const size_t nx,
+                     const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const size_t localNz,
+                     const int activeRank, const int activeSize,
+                     MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
+    const int sendCount = static_cast<int>(localNz * planeCells);
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+
+    if (activeRank == 0) {
+        globalGrid.resize(nz * planeCells);
+        receiveCounts.resize(activeSize);
+        displacements.resize(activeSize);
+
+        const size_t basePlanes = nz / static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
+        const size_t remainder = nz % static_cast<size_t>(activeSize);
+        size_t displacement = 0;
+        for (int rank = 0; rank < activeSize; ++rank) {
+            const size_t rankPlanes = basePlanes
+                + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+            receiveCounts[rank] = static_cast<int>(rankPlanes * planeCells);
+            displacements[rank] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+            displacement += rankPlanes * planeCells;
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localGrid.data() + planeCells, sendCount, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                activeRank == 0 ? globalGrid.data() : nullptr,
+                activeRank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                activeRank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr,
+                MPI_DOUBLE, 0, communicator);
 }

+
+        std::vector<Real> grid1(localCellsWithHalos);
+        std::vector<Real> grid2(localCellsWithHalos);
+
+        if (activeRank == 0) {
+            std::printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
+            std::printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
+            std::printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
+            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+            std::printf("Initializing grid...\n");
+        }
+        initializeGrid(grid1, planeCells, localNz, globalZStart);
+
+        if (activeRank == 0) {
+            std::printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
+        }
+        MPI_Barrier(activeCommunicator);
+        const double startTime = MPI_Wtime();
+
+        for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
+            std::vector<Real>& input = iteration % 2 == 0 ? grid1 : grid2;
+            std::vector<Real>& output = iteration % 2 == 0 ? grid2 : grid1;
+            MPI_Request requests[4];
+            const int requestCount = beginHaloExchange(
+                input, localNz, static_cast<int>(planeCells), activeRank,
+                activeSize, activeCommunicator, requests);
+
+            copyBoundaryValues(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny, nz,
+                               localNz, globalZStart);
+
+            // These planes use only local data, so their work hides halo
+            // communication.  The first and last planes are done after wait.
+            if (localNz > 2) {
+                stencilInteriorPlanes(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny, 2,
+                                      localNz - 1);
+            }
+            if (requestCount != 0) {
+                MPI_Waitall(requestCount, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+            }
+
+            if (globalZStart != 0 && globalZStart + 1 != nz) {
+                stencilInteriorPlanes(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny, 1, 1);
+            }
+            if (localNz > 1 && globalZStart + localNz != nz) {
+                stencilInteriorPlanes(input.data(), output.data(), nx, ny,
+                                      localNz, localNz);
+            }
+        }
+
+        const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - startTime;
+        double elapsed = 0.0;
+        MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+                   activeCommunicator);
+
+        if (activeRank == 0) {
+            const long milliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
+            const double cellUpdates = static_cast<double>(nx - 2)
+                * static_cast<double>(ny - 2) * static_cast<double>(nz - 2)
+                * static_cast<double>(iterations);
+            const double mcups = elapsed > 0.0 ? cellUpdates / elapsed / 1e6 : 0.0;
+            std::printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", milliseconds);
+            std::printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
+        }
+
+        const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = iterations % 2 == 0 ? grid1 : grid2;
+        if (printResults) {
+            std::vector<Real> gatheredGrid;
+            gatherFinalGrid(finalGrid, gatheredGrid, nx, ny, nz, localNz,
+                            activeRank, activeSize, activeCommunicator);
+            if (activeRank == 0) {
+                print_results(gatheredGrid, "Grid");
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (validate) {
+            if (activeRank == 0) {
+                std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            }
+            if (validateResult(finalGrid, localNz, planeCells,
+                               activeCommunicator, activeRank)) {
+                if (activeRank == 0) {
+                    std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+                }
+            } else {
+                if (activeRank == 0) {
+                    std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                }
+                exitCode = 1;
+            }
+        }
+
+        MPI_Comm_free(&activeCommunicator);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return exitCode;
 }

+// Initialize only owned planes.  The two extra planes in each local grid are
+// receive halos and are populated before every iteration.
+void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t planeCells,
+                    const size_t localNz, const size_t globalZStart) {
+    for (size_t localZ = 0; localZ < localNz; ++localZ) {
+        Real* const plane = grid.data() + (localZ + 1) * planeCells;
+        const size_t globalOffset = (globalZStart + localZ) * planeCells;

+            plane[i] = static_cast<Real>((globalOffset + i) % 19);
+        }
+    }

+// Copy the global boundary cells, which retain their values at every step.
+// Interior z planes only need their four x/y faces copied.
+void copyBoundaryValues(const Real* const input, Real* const output,
+                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                        const size_t localNz, const size_t globalZStart) {
+    const size_t planeCells = nx * ny;
+    const size_t rowBytes = nx * sizeof(Real);
+
+    for (size_t localZ = 1; localZ <= localNz; ++localZ) {
+        const size_t globalZ = globalZStart + localZ - 1;
+        const Real* const inPlane = input + localZ * planeCells;
+        Real* const outPlane = output + localZ * planeCells;
+
+        if (globalZ == 0 || globalZ + 1 == nz) {
+            std::memcpy(outPlane, inPlane, planeCells * sizeof(Real));
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        std::memcpy(outPlane, inPlane, rowBytes);
+        std::memcpy(outPlane + (ny - 1) * nx,
+                    inPlane + (ny - 1) * nx, rowBytes);
+        for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {
+            const size_t row = y * nx;
+            outPlane[row] = inPlane[row];
+            outPlane[row + nx - 1] = inPlane[row + nx - 1];
         }
     }
 }

+void stencilInteriorPlanes(const Real* STENCIL_RESTRICT input,

+int beginHaloExchange(std::vector<Real>& input, const size_t localNz,
+                      const int planeCount, const int activeRank,
+                      const int activeSize, MPI_Comm communicator,
+                      MPI_Request requests[4]) {
+    const int lowerRank = activeRank > 0 ? activeRank - 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;
+    const int upperRank = activeRank + 1 < activeSize ? activeRank + 1 : MPI_PROC_NULL;
+    Real* const firstOwnedPlane = input.data() + planeCount;
+    Real* const lastOwnedPlane = input.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localNz) * planeCount;
+    int requestCount = 0;
+
+    // A lower neighbour sends its last plane upward with tag 1, while an
+    // upper neighbour sends its first plane downward with tag 0.
+    if (lowerRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data(), planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, lowerRank, 1,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(firstOwnedPlane, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, lowerRank, 0,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
+    }
+    if (upperRank != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+        MPI_Irecv(input.data() + static_cast<size_t>(localNz + 1) * planeCount,
+                  planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, upperRank, 0, communicator,
+                  &requests[requestCount++]);
+        MPI_Isend(lastOwnedPlane, planeCount, MPI_DOUBLE, upperRank, 1,
+                  communicator, &requests[requestCount++]);
     }
+    return requestCount;
 }
